### [256] [Which LLM is Best for Translating Natural Language Goals to PDDL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18731)
*Tomas Balyo,Lukas Chrpa,G. Michael Youngblood*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过实验评估了大型语言模型在将非正式的游戏测试目标转化为符合PDDL规范的目标方面的性能，发现这些模型在正确性方面表现出色，但仍有改进空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的人机交互在自动化规划中受限于专业知识的要求，而语言模型提供了一种将自然语言测试目标转化为适合经典规划的PDDL目标的新途径。

Method: 设计了一个精心编排的提示模板，整合了多轮迭代实验的见解，评估了六种最新的LLMs在正确性、速度和错误倾向等方面的性能。

Result: 所有模型的正确性均超过92%，Gemini 2.5 Flash在准确性和最小误报方面表现最佳，而GPT-4.1在响应速度方面领先。尽管取得了进展，但模型之间仍存在差异，且偶尔会因为语言模糊和领域表示限制而失败。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，在将自然语言目标与自动化规划管道之间建立桥梁方面取得了显著进展，但还有改进空间，尤其在处理语言模糊和领域知识限制方面。

Abstract: Bridging the gap between human intent and machine execution remains a challenge in automated planning, where expressing goals in formal languages like PDDL restricts accessibility to non-experts. This paper empirically evaluates whether current Large Language Models (LLMs) can reliably translate natural language testing goals, written in informal language by video game testers, into well-formed PDDL targets suitable for classical planning. We present a carefully designed prompt template, integrating insights from iterative experimentation, aimed at maximizing both accuracy and response coherence from multiple state-of-the-art LLMs. Six contemporary models are systematically assessed on correctness, speed, and error tendencies using real-world, domain-specific benchmarks. All models demonstrate high correctness, exceeding 92\%, with Gemini 2.5 Flash achieving the highest accuracy at 96\% and the lowest incidence of false positives, while GPT-4.1 leads in response speed. Despite these advances, critical distinctions exist in model performance, and occasional failures arise from language ambiguity and limitations in domain representation. Our analysis underscores both the significant progress and ongoing gaps in enabling LLMs to act as robust bridges between natural language objectives and automated planning pipelines.

</details>


### [257] [Clueing up LLMs with Tool-Augmented Deductive Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18736)
*Rebecca Ansell,Autumn Toney-Wails*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文实现了一个基于文本的多智能体版经典版推理游戏Clue，用以评估多步骤推理能力。通过引入结构化可能性矩阵工具，将隐含游戏状态转化为显式表示，帮助保持推理连贯性和记忆，从而提升智能体的推理质量和任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管大语言模型（LLMs）取得了进展，但在长时间的逻辑推理任务中的推理一致性和完整性仍存在挑战。本文提出一种基于经典推理游戏Clue的多智能体环境，旨在评估多步骤和多代理的推理能力。

Method: 使用六个LLM代理（GPT-4o-mini和Gemini-2.5-Flash）作为玩家参与轮流游戏，通过比较基线性能与引入工具辅助的性能来评估工具增强在策略推理环境中的作用。

Result: 结果显示工具辅助的方式显著提升了智能体的推理质量和任务成功 rates，其中，结构化可能性矩阵能够有效保持推理连贯性和记忆。

Conclusion: 本文通过新方法（结构化可能性矩阵工具）显著改善了LLMs在多步骤推理任务中的表现，进一步提高了智能体处理复杂策略推理任务的能力。

Abstract: Despite recent advances in large language models (LLMs), performing logically consistent deductive reasoning over extended interactions remains challenging. Tasks that require integrating evidence across multiple reasoning steps, maintaining consistency with prior inferences, and updating beliefs under new constraints can surface limitations in current models while providing a useful testbed for evaluating reasoning enhancements. In this paper, we implement a text-based, multi-agent version of the classic board game Clue as an environment to evaluate multi-step, agentic deductive reasoning. In this setting, agents must infer hidden information from a sequence of observations, maintain consistency across turns, and reason over an evolving set of logical constraints. We instantiate six LLM-based agents (GPT-4o-mini and Gemini-2.5-Flash) as players that engage in turn-based gameplay; using three agents per model family, we establish baseline performance across repeated games. We then introduce a tool-augmented approach in which a structured possibility matrix converts implicit game state from generated reasoning logs into an explicit representation of remaining possibilities. The possibility matrix encodes extended-turn memory and deductive constraints, offloading these tasks from the agent. We compare this approach against the baseline to evaluate how tool augmentation supports reasoning quality and task success for autonomous agents in a strategic reasoning environment.

</details>


### [258] [Version- and Scope-Aware Question Answering over Normative Documents: A Deployed System and an End-to-End Evaluation at Production Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18769)
*Liuyin Wang,Shuaipeng Jin,Jiwei Shi,Jensen Hsu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究评估了托管检索服务与遵循规则系统的性能差异，后者在生成之前解决了版本和范围问题。托管服务的总体得分为88.1，而遵循规则系统的得分为97.7，差距为9.6分。研究结果已公开，并已商业化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于评估当前使用托管检索服务解决复杂法律问题的局限性，并探索一种更加严格的方法来解决版本、适用范围等问题，以提高法律检索系统的准确性和可靠性。

Method: 研究通过对比分析两种系统的表现：一种是使用托管检索服务（即“上传文档并提问”的默认方式），另一种是遵循明确规则的系统。研究团队使用了一套经过分层抽样的200个问题进行评估，并设定了一套公开的评分规则来比较两者的性能。

Result: 实验结果显示，遵循规则的系统的得分显著高于托管检索服务。具体而言，遵循规则系统在经过详细的版本和适用性分析后，能够更准确地回答法律问题，其总体得分为97.7，而托管服务的得分为88.1，分差达到9.6分。

Conclusion: 研究的结论是，虽然托管检索服务在工程实现上更快捷，但为了确保法律问题回答的准确性和可靠性，一种更加结构化和细致的方法是必要的。研究结果还表明，这个遵循规则的系统已经在实际中得到了成功应用，并已达到每天10万次调用的规模。

Abstract: Correctly answering a question grounded in normative documents often depends on information outside any single passage: whether the retrieved document is the version currently in force; whether it applies to the jurisdiction, subject (such as an institution or applicant), and date at issue; and whether each normative claim can be traced to its supporting source text. Hosted retrieval services have substantially lowered the engineering cost of building an initial system over such corpora, making "upload the documents and ask" a common default. We evaluate this default on approximately 73,000 candidate normative documents supplied to a production deployment. The evaluation uses a stratified sample of 200 questions from our published benchmark, with a gold source document for every question; the released sampling rule reads no system outputs or scores. We compare the hosted service with a governed system that resolves version and scope through explicit rules before generation. The governed system scored 97.7 overall, while the hosted service scored 88.1, a gap of 9.6 points computed from unrounded means. The question set, the answer text evaluated for both systems, the scores, and the scripts used to reproduce the reported benchmark statistics are public. The governed configuration has operated as a commercial product since January 2026 and serves 1,126 registered users; named customer organizations include Zhipu AI and Lecheng Health. By mid-April 2026, it had reached roughly 100,000 calls per workday.

</details>


### [259] [CERA-MoA: Co-Evolving Routing Mechanisms with Continually Learning LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18779)
*Jiaxuan Jiang,Liyuan He,Zhixuan Fang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CERA-MoA（Co-Evolving Router with continually learning Agents for Mixture-of-Agents）是一种迭代的强化学习框架，其中动态路由与独立的代理人策略协同进化。该框架通过预测熟悉的估算器利用中间层隐藏状态来评估代理人的语义能力，并基于这些熟悉度分数，动态激活相应的最小代理子集，以在任务性能和效率之间达到平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的Mixture-of-Agents (MoA)模型在查询路由和代理微调之间存在分离的处理过程，这种分离限制了它们对不断演化的代理能力的响应能力。CERA-MoA旨在通过让动态路由和独立的代理策略协同进化来解决这个问题，从而允许路由策略适应培训后的代理能力的变化，并促使代理通过数据驱动的方式实现协同的专业化。

Method: CERA-MoA采用了一种迭代的强化学习框架，其中包含预测熟悉度的估算器来利用中间层的隐藏状态，评估代理人的语义能力。然后，基于这些熟悉度分数，CERA-MoA采用一个累积阈值自适应路由机制，动态激活最适合当前任务的最小代理子集。此外，通过有针对性地向进化能力的代理提供训练样本，CERA-MoA促进代理能力的分化。

Result: 在不同领域的广泛实验中，CERA-MoA的表现超越了现有的静态代理路由和固定工作流微调基准。

Conclusion: CERA-MoA为Mixture-of-Agents模型提供了一种新的视角，通过让路由和代理策略协同进化的方式，实现了对不断演化的代理能力的适应，并在任务性能和效率之间达到了更好的平衡。

Abstract: Current Mixture-of-Agents (MoA) paradigms generally treat query routing and agent fine-tuning as separate processes, limiting their ability to respond to evolving agent capabilities. This disconnect prevents routing strategies from adapting to evolving agent capabilities during post-training and prevents agents from achieving synergistic data-driven specialization. To resolve this, we introduce CERA-MoA (Co-Evolving Router with continually learning Agents for Mixture-of-Agents), an iterative reinforcement learning framework where the dynamic router and independent agent policies co-evolve. We design a predictive familiarity estimator that leverages mid-layer hidden states to evaluate semantic competence among agents, avoiding the overhead of full rollouts. Based on these familiarity scores, a cumulative-threshold adaptive routing mechanism dynamically activates a tailored minimal agent subset, achieving a trade-off between task performance and efficiency. By proactively allocating targeted training samples to agents based on their evolving competence, CERA-MoA promotes capability differentiation. Extensive experiments across various domains demonstrate that CERA-MoA outperforms state-of-the-art static-agent routing and fix-workflow fine-tuning baselines.

</details>


### [260] [Compositional Policy Violations: When Step-Level Compliance Fails In Agentic AI Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18820)
*Ashwini Kurady,Sri Sai Charith Grandhi,Rajesh Gupta,Sumit Mamoria*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探讨了由多个步骤组成的流程可能会违反组织的整体政策的问题，并提出了一种新的治理方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着自动化流程在受监管环境中做出的决策越来越重要，管理这些流程的治理策略显得不够完善。

Method: 作者首先定义了四种类型的组合政策违规（CPV），包括权限渗透、阈值漂洗、累积和违反以及上下文塌陷。然后设计了一种基于溯源的运行时架构，可以重新计算原始数据而非管道衍生的数据来评估策略。

Result: 作者通过基于溯源的架构能够有效检测和修复由多个步骤组成的流程中违反整体政策的问题。

Conclusion: 本文针对传统治理机制不能检测和修复跨步骤的整体政策违规的问题提供了解决方案，并提出了实践性较强的基于溯源的运行时架构。

Abstract: Agentic workflows now make consequential decisions in regulated settings, and the governance placed around them is almost entirely step-scoped: input-output classifiers, per turn rails, and span-level evaluators. The policies organizations actually hold, such as referral thresholds, authority limits, and review requirements, are properties of the whole execution rather than of any one step. This mismatch admits a failure mode we call a Compositional Policy Violation (CPV): every individual step passes its own check while the composed execution violates the governing policy. A predicate over a single step cannot evaluate a property that step does not determine, so no improvement in the accuracy of the step-scoped monitors detects this class. We define CPVs as the failure of step-level compliance to compose, and present a taxonomy of four types: Authority Creep, Threshold Laundering, Cumulative Sum Violation, and Context Collapse. We show that the correct repair for each class is dictated by where the guarded quantity mutates. We then introduce a provenance-aware runtime architecture that evaluates policies over complete execution traces, recomputing guarded quantities from raw provenance rather than the pipeline's derived representation.

</details>


### [261] [Infinite-Parameter LLMs: Generating and Adapting Weights from Live Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18842)
*Jinli Hu,Ross M. Clarke,Yichuan Zhang,José Miguel Hernández-Lobato*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种Infinite-Parameter LLM架构，通过实时数据生成前馈权重，从而实现从实时交互中学习。这种方法与传统的基于MoE的模型不同，它在会话过程中不断更新权重，并通过贝叶斯方法保持动态的权重。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的语言模型通常依赖静态的训练数据，在线交互所产生的有用信息并未被利用。本文旨在提出一种新架构，能够实时从用户交互中获取反馈，并将这些反馈集成到模型权重中。

Method: 一种基于MoE的Infinite-Parameter LLM架构，利用紧凑的超网络将运行时数据映射到共享基网络上，生成实时相关的前馈权重。通过贝叶斯方法更新权重的潜在代码，使其在会话过程中动态调整。

Result: 该架构能够在会话进行时不断更新和生成权重，从而实现从实时交互中学习。与传统的仅在会话开始时一次性读取上下文的模型相比，该方法在计算成本、上下文窗口大小和跨回合一致性方面具有优势。

Conclusion: 本文提出的方法展示了更好的可扩展性和适应性，可以有效利用在线交互产生的数据，提高模型的性能。未来的评估表明，与基于上下文的学习和检索相比，新的架构能够获得更好的效果。

Abstract: The scaling laws hold that a language model grows more capable with more parameters and more training data, and Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures have ridden these laws to remarkable results, activating only a fraction of an enormous stored parameter bank for each token. That success is built on static pretraining data. A deployed model faces a different world, where much of the data that would make it more useful is not in its training set but in the live interaction it is currently handling, such as the facts a user supplies or the corrections they give. A conventional model cannot learn from this data, because its weights are frozen after training. Instead, the knowledge and behaviour supplied at run time are placed in the prompt, by retrieval or instruction, and re-read on every request only to be discarded once the request ends. We ask how an architecture could learn from live interaction by writing it into its weights. Taking inspiration from MoE, we propose the \textbf{Infinite-Parameter LLM}. A compact hypernetwork turns the data given at run time into a low-rank modulation of a shared base network, so the feed-forward weights are generated from live data rather than stored in a fixed bank. Where prior weight generators read the context once and freeze, we carry a Bayesian belief over the generator's latent code and update it online, so the effective weight is re-derived from that evolving belief as the session proceeds rather than fixed after one read. The stored footprint stays fixed, yet the weights the model can compile are effectively infinite. For the knowledge and behaviour supplied at run time, carrying them in the weights rather than the prompt is amortized in compute, frees the context window, persists across turns, and can generalise better than in-context use. We specify an evaluation protocol that tests exactly this against in-context learning and retrieval.

</details>


### [262] [Suppressed, Not Erased: A Representational Trace of Edited Facts Survives Even Weight-Free Knowledge Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18985)
*Priyansh Srivastava,Romit Chatterjee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究发现，在成功编辑事实后，模型中仍保留了原始对象的可线性解码信息，尤其是在GRACE编辑器下，即使没有对基模型权重进行更新，原始关联仍然存在。这表明编辑操作更多抑制而不是完全抹去原始关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的知识编辑基准仅验证编辑模型是否能够在附近编辑提示下生成新事实，但无法评估原始信息有多少残留于模型中。本文提出使用线性踪迹探针直接研究残留知识，通过观察编辑后模型中原始对象是否仍然可解码来衡量编辑效果。

Method: 使用线性踪迹探针评估三种不同编辑器（ROME、约束微调和记忆基向量编辑器GRACE）对50个CounterFact编辑效果的模型中残留的原始信息。

Result: 在GPT-2-XL模型上，三种编辑器均导致原始对象线性解码的准确率远高于随机水平（ROME为0.96，约束微调为0.86，GRACE为0.79，与随机水平0.50相比）。尤其是在GRACE编辑器下，不改变基模型权重，仅通过外部存储器便可使原始信息仍然存在于模型的嵌入空间中。

Conclusion: 研究表明，知识编辑即使在行为上看似成功，更多抑制了原始关联而并未彻底抹去。此外，在使用的重学节省指标在本研究中未能提供可靠结果，认为这是方法论上的一个负面结果。

Abstract: Knowledge-editing benchmarks certify local correctness, whether an edited model produces the new fact on near-edit prompts but not how much of the original fact remains decodable inside the model. We study residual knowledge directly with a linear trace probe: after editing a fact, we ask whether the original object is still recoverable from the model's hidden states. On GPT-2-XL, across three mechanistically distinct editors applied to 50 CounterFact edits, the original object remains linearly decodable well above chance after a successful edit (probe accuracy 0.96 for ROME, 0.86 for constrained fine-tuning, and 0.79 for the memory-based editor GRACE, against a chance level of 0.50; all edits reach 100% generation-based success). The GRACE result is the most informative: GRACE changes zero base-model weights, overriding the fact through an external memory, yet the original object is still decodable from the underlying network, so the residual trace cannot be attributed to an incomplete weight update. We read this as evidence that editing, even when behaviorally successful, suppresses rather than erases the original association in representational space. We also report a relearning-savings instrument that did not behave reliably in our setting and discuss why; we treat it as a negative methodological result rather than evidence. Code and data are released.

</details>


### [263] [Function Lives Where Variance Doesn't: Task-Weighted Charts of a Language Model's Computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18989)
*Alexandre Quemy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究发现语言模型的实际计算维度依赖于特定的功能，对于下一词预测任务，大约70-90%的剩余流宽度足以保持5%以内的困惑度，这主要由罕见的语言尾部决定。模型内部的不确定性读取维度和完整预测分布所需的维度数量不同，且随模型深度增加而增长。功能匹配的图表训练优于基于方差或最优线性压缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究探讨了语言模型计算维度的实际使用情况，以及不同任务所需的维度数。通过对比传统的基于方差的压缩方法和功能匹配的图表训练方法，证明了直接针对特定任务进行优化的效果更好。

Method: 该研究使用了多个不同规模的语言模型，并通过任务加权图表的方法进行了实验。实验涉及的功能为下一词预测任务。通过分析每个模型中不同维度的激活量和功能实现情况，确定了模型内部特定功能所需的最小维度数。

Result: 研究发现，对于下一词预测任务，大约70-90%的剩余流宽度足以保持5%以内的困惑度，而这主要归因于语言中的罕见部分。此外，模型内部的不确定性和完整预测分布所需的维度数量不同，且随着模型深度增加而增长。基于功能的图表训练能够更有效地保存模型预测。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了语言模型计算维度是功能导向的，对于特定任务的关键维度数远小于整体维度。功能匹配的图表训练方法在未来模型压缩和优化中具有潜在的应用前景。

Abstract: How many dimensions does a language model's computation actually use? The question is ill-posed until one names a functional. Task-weighted charts make it well-posed: low-dimensional coordinate systems fit against a chosen functional of the representation, under the functional's own metric, turning distillation into plain least squares. Across six models from three families, spanning 70m to 7B parameters, next-token prediction needs 70--90% of the residual stream's width to stay within 5% of intact perplexity, a width consumed by the rare tail of language, and the variance profile predicts none of it: two directions carry 90% of GPT-2's activation variance and almost none of its function. Dimension is per-functional: the model's own uncertainty reads from six coordinates where the full predictive distribution needs hundreds; and it grows with depth. The dissociation is exploitable: when only a few dimensions can be kept, charts trained under the functional's metric preserve the model's predictions better than variance-based or optimal linear compression.

</details>


### [264] [Lost in Perception: Isolating Perceptual and Reasoning Failures in Multimodal Physics and Geometry Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18991)
*Raj Jaiswal,Sree Krishna Uppalapati,Dhruvkumar Patel,Ria Khatoniar,Tanuja Ganu,Rajiv Ratn Shah*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究设计了一个多任务诊断实验，通过物理和几何基准测试来解析模型在感知和推理过程中的失败点。结果显示，错误的图示解读会对性能产生负面影响，而修正的图示能显著提升某些模型的准确率。不同领域的推理错误与感知失败有关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大多数LLM将感知和推理视为单一过程，忽视了他们之间的区分。因此，本研究旨在通过独立评估感知和推理的效果，找到模型在科学推理任务中的真正瓶颈。

Method: 研究设计了一个包含五个任务的多任务诊断实验，涵盖物理和几何领域。实验对比了不同类型输入（如原始图像、人类自动生成的图示）对模型性能的影响，以及经过修正的图示如何改善模型的表现。

Result: 研究发现，错误的图示解读会对模型的整体性能造成负面影响，即使对于某些仅基于文本输入正确解决的问题也是如此。修正后的图示显著提升了某些模型的准确性，尤其是那些感知受限的模型。此外，不同领域的推理错误类型不同，物理领域的错误通常涉及计算错误，而几何领域的错误则涉及概念性误解。

Conclusion: 研究指出，感知缺陷和推理缺陷在科学理由任务中是相互独立的。尽管InternS1-mini拥有大量科学预训练和思考能力，但在所有测试任务中依然表现最差，表明模型在推理过程中存在严重问题，且这些问题在科学预训练和内部思考能力上存在不足。

Abstract: Multimodal LLMs report strong performance on scientific reasoning benchmarks, yet most treat perception and reasoning as a single measurable process. We introduce a five-task diagnostic experiment across physics and geometry benchmarks that isolates failures to perception, reasoning, or both. Incorrect diagram interpretation degrades performance even on problems models solve correctly from text alone, and accuracy generally rises from raw images to human-authored captions. Recovery under corrected captions is high for some models, separating perception-blocked failures from genuine reasoning bottlenecks. Which reasoning error follows a perception failure depends on domain: physics failures resolve into calculation errors, geometry into conceptual misapplication. As a discussion beyond our core experiments, InternS1-mini, despite heavy scientific pretraining and thinking capabilities, falls below the weakest model from experiments on every task, with reasoning traces frequently truncating before completion.

</details>


### [265] [Compiled Agency: Frontier General-Purpose Coding Agents Build Winning Game Players from Bare Interaction - from Flappy Bird to StarCraft II and Civilization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18996)
*Joey Xiao,Haonan Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种名为Gauntlet的框架，能够将游戏从小型街机移植到全规模商业游戏，通过一个非常基础的接口，直接从游戏描述、原始观察/动作接口以及空的策略文件开始，无需任何策略、算法或架构。通过单一自主会话，该框架可以生成独立的游戏控制器，并在测试中表现出显著的进步，甚至可以在未见过的种子上完成完整的游戏。这代表了首次无需每轮模型调用和手工工艺战术层，即在某些类型的游戏中实现独立运行的胜利。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管现有语言模型在游戏代理方面取得了显著进展，但仍难以将知识转化为有效的游戏策略。因此，提出一个新的框架Gauntlet，旨在测试新型语言代理系统能否从零开始构建自己的游戏策略并获得成功。

Method: 研究使用了一种开发冻结评估框架Gauntlet，该框架通过提供游戏描述、原始观察和动作接口以及一个空的策略文件，利用语言模型从零开始开发游戏策略。

Result: 实验结果表明，Gauntlet框架能够在一个未公布的随机生成类 roguelike游戏中取得0-86％的成功率，特别是在最新一代系统中表现出显著进步。在更大的规模上，通过原始API编译的控制器击败了公平StarCraft II内置AI和两个作弊变体。单会话程序在不同种子的《文明》游戏中实现了全面征服，展示出具备对远期策略跟踪的能力。

Conclusion: 研究结果支持了语言模型能够在不依赖用户干预的情况下构建自己的游戏策略，并在特定类型的游戏上实现了独立胜利。这种能力被命名为“编译代理”，展示了模型在长期策略跟踪方面的潜力。

Abstract: LLM agents have repeatedly struggled to convert knowledge of a game into competent play, even when researchers build the agent around the model - supplying perception, memory, skill libraries, planners, or executable-policy scaffolds. Rapid progress in coding agents raises two sharper questions: can frontier models now win games at all, and can they win them unaided, building the entire player themselves? We introduce Gauntlet, a develop-freeze-evaluate framework that ports games from small arcades to full commercial-scale titles, behind one deliberately bare contract: a general-purpose coding agent receives a game description, a raw observation/action interface, and an empty policy file - no strategy, no algorithm, no architecture. In a single autonomous session the agent experiments with the live game and engineers a standalone controller; we freeze the result and score it on held-out instances with zero model calls during play. On an unpublished procedural roguelike, held-out success spans 0-86 percent and exposes a sharp generational threshold: every observed session of a newest-generation system outperforms the best session of its predecessor. At full-game scale, a compiled raw-API controller defeats every fair StarCraft II built-in AI and two cheating variants, and single-session programs win complete games of Civilization (Freeciv) by total conquest on held-out seeds. Though at modest rates against novice AI, this is a first: no prior language-agent system had won full games of this genre standalone, without per-turn model calls and a hand-crafted tactical layer. Frontier coding agents begin to track long-horizon strategy. The frozen programs are inspectable. We call this capability compiled agency: development experience compiled into a persistent executable agent whose architecture is built by the model.

</details>


### [266] [MUSE: Benchmarking Large Vision-Language Models on Multi-Modal Understanding in Situated Education](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19088)
*Luyao Zhu,Xun Wei Yee,Wei Li,Mun Thye Mak,Wee Siong Ng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MUSE是一个基准，用于评估大型视觉语言模型在艺术图像理解方面的表现，特别是在情境化的教育应用中。它通过独特的内容和任务设计，揭示了现有模型在情感理解和联想推理方面的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的评估基准主要集中在现实世界图像或特定领域的教育推理上，缺乏对艺术教育内容的全面覆盖。MUSE旨在填补这一空白，提供一个更为全面的评估基准。

Method: MUSE通过将图像标注与问题生成分离，实现任务多样性和难度可控性，同时减少标注工作量。它包含了视觉感知、语义和情感解读、文化理解及联想推理等多个任务，涉及多样化的艺术图像。

Result: MUSE的评估结果显示，开源和商用模型在情感理解和联想推理上的表现存在显著差异。此外，分析还指出了模型在可信的多模态教育应用中的一般失败模式和关键挑战。

Conclusion: MUSE旨在为多模态理解在情境教育应用中的进步设定一个公认的标准基准，以促进相关研究的发展。

Abstract: Large vision-language models have achieved remarkable progress in multi-modal understanding, yet their capabilities in educational settings remain insufficiently evaluated. In AI-assisted language learning, models must interpret artistic imagery, understand its semantic, affective, and cultural content, and reason about visual context to support meaningful interaction. However, existing benchmarks primarily focus on real-world images or domain-specific educational reasoning, providing limited coverage of artistic educational content. To address this gap, we introduce MUSE, a benchmark for evaluating large vision-language models on artistic image understanding in situated educational applications. MUSE decouples image annotation from question generation, enabling diverse tasks with controllable difficulty while reducing annotation effort. It comprises twelve tasks spanning visual perception, semantic and affective interpretation, culture understanding, and compositional reasoning, together with diverse artistic images deliberately curated to center Singaporean and Southeast Asian multicultural contexts alongside Western art traditions, covering multiple themes and difficulty levels. Evaluation of open-source and proprietary models reveals substantial disparities across capability dimensions, particularly in affective interpretation and compositional reasoning. Our analysis further identifies common failure modes and key challenges for developing trustworthy multi-modal models for education. We hope MUSE will serve as a standardized benchmark for advancing multi-modal understanding in situated educational applications.

</details>


### [267] [Flag Game: A Toy Model for Mechanistic Swarm Interpretability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19124)
*Elizabeth Pavlova,Hidenori Tanaka*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究引入了旗标游戏作为玩具模型，探索集体信念形成的机制。研究展示了群体规模、社交意识以及团队多样性如何影响群体表现，并提出了社会电路归属和统计力学理论来解释集体信念崩溃和极化现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI代理出现新的协调行为，它们开始带来关键的安全风险。研究动机在于理解这种现象背后的心理机制，特别是如何通过社交证据和群体内多样化相互作用形成集体信念。

Method: 研究团队设计了一个简单的旗标游戏模型，通过模拟和分析小规模到大规模群体下的行为变化，来探讨集体信念形成的机制。研究还引入了社会电路归属方法，并发展了统计力学理论来解释大规模群体的集体行为。

Result: 研究发现在小规模群体中，集体信念可能迅速塌陷，但在群体规模增大时，极化的集体信念会导致表现下降，但也能增加观点多样性。研究还通过社会电路归属法和统计力学理论验证了这些发现。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了集体信念形成的复杂性和多样性，强调了社交交流和群体组织结构对群体行为的重要影响。这些发现是对群体智能和机制蚁群可解释性的初步探索，为进一步理解复杂系统中的集体行为提供了科学依据。

Abstract: Emergent coordinated behaviors of AI agents are starting to present critical safety risks. A key phenomenon driving these behaviors is the rapid formation and spread of beliefs about the world, and mechanistic understanding is crucial for collective alignment. To this end, we introduce the Flag Game, a toy model for studying the mechanisms of collective belief formation. Concretely, a hidden country flag defines the ground truth, and each bounded agent directly observes only a private crop but can exchange beliefs and weigh social evidence from peers. Despite its simplicity, the Flag Game reproduces rich collective phenomenology: non-monotonic scaling of performance with population size, accuracy gains from social-awareness prompting and team diversity, and strong effects of organizational structure. In particular, we identify that collective belief collapse at small population sizes turns into collective belief polarization as the population grows. This polarization causes the performance decline at large population sizes, but creates diversity in collective beliefs. Finally, we dissect the mechanisms underlying collective belief collapse and polarization with two complementary approaches. We first introduce social circuit attribution, a technique to predict which agent, and what view, matters most to collective dynamics, and verify its predictions by causal interventions on agents, tracing how agent patching changes collective outcomes. However, the efficacy of causal interventions on agents decreases as the population grows. We therefore develop a statistical mechanical theory for larger populations and verify that it matches the empirical phase diagram. Together, these results take a first step toward mechanistic swarm interpretability, a science of how the properties of individual agents and their communication give rise to emergent collective behavior.

</details>


### [268] [Cognitive Extensions for Dual-Process Language Agents: Memory and Self-Reflection in Interactive Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19128)
*João Meneses dos Santos,Arlindo L. Oliveira*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究通过在SwiftSage代理上添加记忆模块（AMM）和反思模块（SRM），提高了代理在长时间交互环境中的表现，特别是提高了成功率和有效步骤效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于在互动环境中，语言代理需要追踪长时间的状态、执行有效动作并从失败的步骤中恢复，因此现有代理表现仍然脆弱。该研究旨在通过添加记忆和反思模块来增强代理的能力，以应对这些挑战。

Method: 研究人员扩展了SwiftSage代理，并在此基础上添加了两个模块：自适应记忆模块（AMM）和自我反思模块（SRM）。AMM用于选择性地存储和检索相关记忆，而SRM则在执行过程中提供约束和纠正干预。

Result: 经过四种配置——基线、基线+AMM、基线+SRM和完整系统——的测试，完整系统获得了最高的平均最终得分（64.62）、成功率（43.17%）和成功步骤效率（19.33 步）。特别是，SRM成为最强的独立贡献者。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，在此环境中，执行时间和控制是主要瓶颈，而情景记忆则在运行稳定后变得最有用。

Abstract: Language agents remain brittle in interactive environments, where success requires long-horizon state tracking, valid action execution, and recovery from failed steps. We extend SwiftSage, a dual-process agent that combines a fast action proposer with a slower planner, using two modular cognitive extensions: an Adaptive Memory Module (AMM) for salience-gated episodic storage and trigger-driven retrieval, and a Self-Reflection Module (SRM) for bounded execution-time validation and corrective intervention. Both modules are implemented as feature-flagged extensions over the same execution substrate, enabling controlled ablations on ScienceWorld. Across four configurations---baseline, baseline+AMM, baseline+SRM, and the full system---the full system achieves the best mean final score (64.62), success rate (43.17%), and successful-step efficiency (19.33 steps), while SRM is the strongest standalone contributor. The results suggest that execution-time control is the dominant bottleneck in this setting, while episodic memory becomes most useful once the runtime loop is stabilized.

</details>


<div id='cs.AR'></div>

# cs.AR [[Back]](#toc)

### [269] [REQAP: Resilient Weight Packing and Quantization for Edge DNN Acceleration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17555)
*Mahdi Taheri,Samira Nazari,Mubassher Ansari,Ali Azarpeyvand,Mohsen Afsharchi,Maksim Jenihhin,Christian Herglotz*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对Systolic-array基DNN加速器的可靠性意识量化权重打包方法，利用精度敏感的混合精度量化框架和确定性的寄存器级打包策略，实现SIMD-within-a-register (SWAR) 并行执行，并通过关键层的位级冗余保护提高硬件故障的鲁棒性，实验结果表明，该方法可实现高达62%的内存减少和56%的MAC运算减少，同时提高了准确性的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在边缘计算环境中部署深度神经网络模型时，为了提高资源利用效率，需要进行模型压缩，但又要保证在硬件故障多发的环境中模型的可靠性。

Method: 本文提出了一种基于灵敏度驱动的混合精度量化框架和确定性寄存器级打包策略的方法，该方法在量化网络权重的同时，通过合并不相关操作对来减少数据所需的总位宽，从而实现SIMD-within-a-register (SWAR) 并行执行。此外，还通过复制关键层的最显著位进入冗余空间来增强硬件故障的鲁棒性。

Result: 仿真结果表明，论文提出的方法在AlexNet，VGG-11和ResNet-18模型上能够分别实现高达62%的内存减少和56%的MAC运算减少，同时提高了模型在故障注入条件下的准确度鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该研究为在故障多发的硬件环境中高效部署深度神经网络模型提供了一种有效的方案。

Abstract: Efficient deployment of Deep Neural Networks (DNNs) on edge accelerators requires aggressive model compression while maintaining reliability in fault-prone hardware environments. This paper presents a reliability-aware quantized weight packing methodology for systolic-array-based DNN accelerators. A sensitivity-driven mixed-precision quantization framework assigns layer-wise bit-widths according to accuracy impact while enforcing symmetric precision between weights and activations. A deterministic register-level packing strategy consolidates multiple heterogeneous operand pairs into fixed-width register words, enabling SIMD-within-a-register (SWAR) style parallel execution that reduces both memory footprint and execution cycles. To improve resilience against hardware faults, selective bit-level protection replicates the most significant bits (MSBs) of critical layers into unused register space, achieving TMR-style protection with minimal overhead. A systolic-array simulation framework is developed to evaluate the proposed packing and fault-tolerance mechanisms under realistic execution conditions. Simulations in AlexNet, VGG-11, and ResNet-18 demonstrate up to 62% memory reduction and up to 56% reduction in Multiply-Accumulate (MAC) operations, while significantly improving accuracy resilience under fault injection compared to baseline and fully protected models.

</details>


### [270] [WARD: Runtime Workload-Adaptive Vision TRansformer Framework for Dependable Edge AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17556)
*Mahdi Taheri,Pramit Kumar Bhaduri,Mohammad Masoumi,Ali Mahani*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: WARD 是一种新的运行时自适应视觉变换器框架，结合了通道级子网络分割、可靠性感知连续学习和动态运行模式调度，以适应运行时条件的变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法假设静态运行条件，而持续学习技术忽略了实时计算过程中的硬件故障，WARD 框架旨在解决这些不足，适用于动态变化的边沿AI应用和计算加速器的自适应可靠性管理。

Method: WARD 通过将视觉变换器划分为两个独立的子网络，在四种不同的运行模式下执行，这些模式动态调整计算成本和可靠性，同时确保实时推理不间断。每个子网络在不同模式下运行不同的操作。

Result: 实验结果显示，在高比特错误率的情况下，提出的拆分架构的网络级失败率仅为1.79%。硬件实现的面积开销不到5%，且支持几周期内的运行模式转换，表明可适应的可靠性管理可以被集成到可编程边沿AI加速器中，且实现成本低廉。

Conclusion: 这项研究引入了 WARD，通过自适应机制提高了边沿AI应用的可靠性，并针对实际部署进行了验证。

Abstract: Edge-deployed AI operate under dynamically changing power budgets, reliability requirements, and input distributions, requiring continuous adaptation. Such conditions arise in long-running edge AI applications, including autonomous systems, industrial monitoring, and satellite onboard intelligence. Existing fault-tolerant methods assume static operating conditions, whereas continual learning techniques neglect concurrent hardware faults during online adaptation. Moreover, the practical deployment of runtime-adaptive reliability frameworks on programmable AI accelerators remains largely unexplored.
  This paper presents WARD, a runtime-adaptive Vision Transformer framework that combines channel-wise subnetwork partitioning, reliability-aware continual learning, and dynamic operating-mode scheduling to jointly optimize performance, fault tolerance, and adaptation according to runtime conditions. Two physically isolated subnetworks execute under four operating modes (i.e. Full-Precision Mode, Low-Power Mode, High-Reliability Mode, and Adaptive Mode) that dynamically adjust computational cost and reliability while ensuring uninterrupted inference for real-time requirements. To validate the practical deployability of the proposed framework, WARD is implemented on a lightweight FPGA-based accelerator extended with runtime hardware support for mode scheduling and resource management. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed split architecture achieves a network-level failure rate of only 1.79% under high Bit Error Rates. The hardware implementation incurs less than 5% area overhead and supports runtime mode transitions within few clock cycles, demonstrating that adaptive reliability management can be integrated into programmable edge AI accelerators with negligible implementation overhead.

</details>


### [271] [Lyapunov-based analysis of functional stability for edge computing systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17569)
*Oleksii S. Bychkov*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 该论文将功能稳定性与直接李雅普诺夫方法联系起来，通过有界区域的正不变性和极限有界性、输入到状态稳定性等概念对其进行描述，同时处理了服务迁移和节点故障切换的切换动力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在边缘计算环境中，提出功能稳定性作为单个服务的可靠性概念，以解决传统系统级别的二元工作/失败评估和边缘系统中具体的边缘调度器、迁移策略或分布式推理服务的可靠性验证问题。

Method: 该方法通过使用直接李雅普诺夫方法来定义功能稳定性，引入了强形式和弱形式的区别，并通过共同的李雅普诺夫函数处理切换动力学。

Result: 论文确定了功能稳定性的具体形式，并提供了恢复时间的分析性上界，并讨论了目标受限的边缘工作负载下的有限时限变体。

Conclusion: 通过将功能稳定性与直接李雅普诺夫方法相结合，本论文为边缘计算服务的功能稳定性提供了实用的分析工具。

Abstract: Functional stability has been proposed as a per-function reliability concept for edge computing, in which the unit of analysis is the individual service rather than the whole system and the binary working/failed evaluation is replaced by a continuous quality function with per-function thresholds. Verifying these properties for a concrete edge orchestrator, migration policy, or distributed inference service requires a constructive method. This article connects functional stability to the direct Lyapunov method. The strong form is characterized through positive invariance of the admissible region; the weak form is connected to uniform ultimate boundedness and input-to-state stability with analytical recovery-time bounds. Switched dynamics from service migration and node failover are treated via common Lyapunov functions. Finite-horizon variants address mission-bounded edge workloads.

</details>


### [272] [FAME: An FPGA-Based Platform for Approximate Multipliers Evaluation with Pattern-Guided DNN Retraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17730)
*Rappy Saha,Nima Amirafshar,Jude Haris,Nima Taherinejad,José Cano*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: FAME通过FPGA实现近似乘法器的直接硬件仿真，解决了由于使用LUT在CPU/GPU上进行仿真导致的成本高昂的问题，提高了评估效率并有效提升了准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提出FAME平台为了降低评估近似乘法器准确性的成本，解决由于硬件仿真慢和准确性下降带来的挑战。

Method: 利用FPGA的可配置逻辑，直接在硬件中实现近似乘法器，减少LUT仿真耗时；使用模式导向的DNN重训练技术，以乘法器特定的模式指导重训练，恢复潜在的准确度损失。

Result: 在ResNet-18和MobileNetV2两种模型上，FAME实现了高达3.47倍的评估加速，并且重训练技术使准确度提升高达65.5%。

Conclusion: FAME通过硬件加速和重训练技术有效降低了评估近似乘法器的复杂性和成本，同时提高了DNN模型的准确度。

Abstract: Approximate multipliers can reduce hardware area and energy consumption in Deep Neural Network (DNN) inference; however, they introduce computational errors. Assessing the accuracy of numerous approximate multiplier designs across diverse DNN models and large-scale datasets remains challenging due to prohibitive evaluation times. This overhead primarily stems from the slow emulation of approximate multiplier behavior using look-up tables (LUTs) on CPU and GPU platforms. Moreover, the resulting accuracy degradation must be carefully quantified and, if necessary, mitigated (e.g., through retraining), further increasing the overall evaluation cost. To address these challenges, we propose FAME, an FPGA-based platform for evaluating approximate multipliers. The platform exploits the reconfigurable logic of Field-Programmable Gate Arrays (FPGAs) to implement approximate multipliers directly in hardware, eliminating the need for LUT-based emulation on CPU/GPU platforms and thereby enabling efficient DNN inference while significantly reducing evaluation time on large datasets. Furthermore, we introduce a pattern-guided DNN retraining technique to mitigate accuracy degradation induced by approximate multipliers. Specifically, retraining is guided by multiplier-specific patterns to effectively recover potential accuracy losses. We evaluate FAME using two DNN models, ResNet-18 and MobileNetV2, on the ImageNet dataset across 27 approximate multipliers. During inference, our approach achieves up to a 3.47x speedup in approximate multiplier evaluation compared to prior LUT-based emulation methods. Furthermore, the proposed retraining technique improves accuracy by up to 65.5% over existing retraining approaches for the evaluated multipliers. The code is publicly available at: https://github.com/gicLAB/FAME

</details>


### [273] [Demystifying Gate-Level Localization of RTL Trojans](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17922)
*Navid Nader Tehrani,Azadeh Davoodi,Rasit Onur Topaloglu*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种名为LoRD的轻量级启发式方法，通过识别RTL级硬件木马在综合后稳定存在的结构和信号流模式，有效地检测并定位了木马，比基于变压器的机器学习基线和前五名团队平均得分高出约0.4分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 硬件木马是恶意修改，它们在综合后可能被优化隐藏，传统的机器学习方法在没有标签数据和调优成本的情况下效果不佳。为了解决这个问题，本文提出了一种启发式方法LoRD以实现更有效的硬件木马检测。

Method: LoRD通过分析综合后的RTL级电路图中的子图模式，寻找稳定的结构和信号流特征，从而实现木马的检测。该方法不依赖于特定的机器学习特征提取，而是采用轻量级的启发式策略。

Result: LoRD在ICCAD 2025竞赛提供的测试案例中实现了近乎完美的检测和定位，并且与基于变压器的机器学习基线和其他顶级团队相比，平均得分高出约0.4分。

Conclusion: LoRD提供了一种有效的、轻量级的方法来检测硬件木马，这种方法在不依赖特定特征提取的情况下取得了很好的性能，具有实用价值。

Abstract: Hardware Trojans are malicious modifications that compromise functionality or leak sensitive data. They pose a severe threat, particularly when inserted at the Register Transfer Level (RTL). After synthesis, these Trojans are often concealed by optimizations in gate-level netlists. Recent efforts, including the ICCAD 2025 contest, emphasize golden-chip-free detection using machine learning (ML) on labeled netlists. In this work, we show that RTL Trojans exhibit stable structural and signal-flow patterns post-synthesis, enabling effective detection through targeted heuristics rather than generic ML feature learning. We propose LoRD, a lightweight heuristic-based approach that exploits these distinctive subgraph signatures, achieving near-perfect detection and localization on the contest testcases. Com- pared to a transformer-based ML baseline and top five teams, LoRD achieves on-average a score of 2.957 (out of 3) for Trojan- implanted designs without the data and tuning overhead.

</details>


### [274] [VeriBugBench: An Empirically Grounded Framework for Constructing Verilog RTL Debugging Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18022)
*Xiankai Meng,Kejian Feng,Xinlin Zhao,Zhuo Zhang,Yan Lei,Xiaoguang Mao,Jiang Wu*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: VeriBugBench 提供了一种框架，用于通过实证驱动的故障构造、基于LLM的测试平台增强和执行驱动的保留来构建 Verilog RTL 调试基准。该框架涵盖了2,608个可执行的单一故障实例，提高了故障可观察性和覆盖率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有资源通常无法为RTL源级调试提供准确的设计缺陷、变更位置、可执行测试刺激和可再现配置。VeriBugBench旨在填补这一空白，通过实证方法构建基准，以支持更有效的调试工具评估。

Method: 该方法包括构建一个故障库，该库映射到RTL错误修复历史中的频繁出现的多粒度修复模式，生成特定于设计的测试激励，以及使用LLM增强测试平台。利用这些组件，VeriBugBench可以生成具有可再现结果的特定故障实例。

Result: VeriBugBench成功生成了2,608个可执行的单一故障实例，使45个开源项目的总体故障可观察性从36.01%提高到39.54%，并且平均提高了行覆盖率和执行踪迹多样性。

Conclusion: VeriBugBench提供了一个全面的框架，集合了RTL变体、源级真实情况、测试平台和执行产物，可用于评估RTL调试方法。

Abstract: RTL source-level debugging research requires benchmark artifacts that provide faulty designs together with precise change locations, executable test stimuli, and reproducible configurations. Available Verilog resources usually provide only a subset of these elements. We present VeriBugBench, a framework for constructing Verilog RTL debugging benchmarks through empirically grounded fault construction, LLM-based testbench enhancement, and execution-based retention. The mutation library maps recurring, multi-granularity repair patterns observed in RTL bug-fix histories to 19 executable inverse operators. For each project, an LLM generates a design-specific stimulus phase from the clean DUT and original testbench; the phase is composed with the original testbench for candidate execution. Applying the framework to 45 open-source projects yields VeriBugBench-v1.0, with 2,608 executable single-fault instances whose effects are observable at design outputs. Across the 45 projects, the assembled testbenches increase mean project-level fault observability from 36.01% to 39.54% and improve line coverage and execution-trace diversity on average. VeriBugBench provides versioned RTL variants, source-level ground truth, testbenches, and execution artifacts for evaluating RTL debugging methods.

</details>


### [275] [Automated Instruction Encoding Synthesis for Modern GPU ISA Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18662)
*Mingyuan Ma,Hu He*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种自动编码合成功能框架，用于优化现代GPU内核的指令供给路径。该框架通过将指令布局视为受验证指令形式字段规范约束的槽分配问题完成任务。这对于SASS规范下的黑格尔伍尔克内核输入，在变长合成中减少了33%的指令内存足迹，并且固定长度合成减小了16%的解码器面积。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着现代GPU内核对指令供给路径的要求越来越高，传统的固定指令容器可能会导致显著的空置内存空间，因此急需一个解决方案来优化这种内存效率。

Method: 研究人员通过将指令布局视为一个带有约束条件的槽分配问题，提出了一个支持语义字段身份从物理位位置分离的自动编码合成框架。该方法特别适用于那些未被公共格式合同固定的反复出现的解码字段，通过这种方法对NVIDIA SASS进行实例化，实现了指令形式字段规范的机器可读性，并使用CP-SAT算法进行固定长度和变长度编码。

Result: 该框架成功应用于142个黑格尔伍尔克内核输入中，其中变长合成将指令内存足迹减少了33%；相同规格下固定长度合成将解码器面积减少了16%。生成的取指令/解码硬件逻辑在TSMC 22nm工艺下达到了1.5GHz的时钟频率，并且额外的逻辑面积占一代GA100芯片面积的0.12%。在SRAM比较中，这种方法的足迹减少对应于大约9倍的逻辑面积。

Conclusion: 通过该项研究开发的框架显著提高了GPU内的指令处理效率，对于优化现代GPU内核的指令供给路径具有重要意义。

Abstract: Modern GPU kernels increasingly stress the instruction supply path, while fixed instruction containers can leave substantial footprint slack. This paper presents an automated encoding-synthesis framework that treats instruction layout as a constrained slot-assignment problem over a validated instruction-form field specification. The formulation separates semantic field identity from physical bit positions and supports tied, pinned, and free placement constraints, making it applicable when recurring decoded fields are not frozen by a public format contract. We instantiate the framework for NVIDIA SASS: raw public encoding text is normalized into a machine-readable specification, a SASS disassembler is validated against nvdisasm on 3.78M instructions, released as an open benchmark, and CP-SAT synthesis is used for fixed-length and variable-length encodings. On 142 Blackwell kernel inputs, variable-length synthesis reduces instruction footprint by 33%, with comparable reductions after re-synthesis on Ampere and Hopper; fixed-length synthesis on the same specification reduces decoder area by 16% against a decoder generated from the NVIDIA-observed 128-bit layout by the same generator and flow. Generated fetch/decode RTL meets 1.5 GHz in TSMC 22 nm with a replicated area delta of 0.12% of a GA100-class die; a same-node SRAM comparison shows the footprint reduction corresponds to about 9x this added logic in instruction-SRAM bit-cell area.

</details>


### [276] [HBFlex: A Flexible Memory System for Bridging Fine-Grained LLM States and Coarse-Grained HBF Parallel Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18675)
*Shuzhang Zhong,Weikai Xu,Yifan Zhou,Tongbin Zhao,Tenghao Zhao,Yifei Kang,Cunyin Chang,Shu Li,Guangyu Sun,Meng Li*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: HBFlex 是一个全 HBF 存储系统，通过协调优化 KV 读取、写入和回收，提高了平面利用率，减少写读干扰，并降低了有效页面迁移。实验结果显示，HBFlex 在不同配置下相对于 FlashAccel 和 H3 的平均吞吐量提高了 1.58 倍和 3.30 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型的内存需求不断增加，全 HBF（High-Bandwidth Flash）可以提供高内存密度和带宽，但带来了 KV 读取平衡性、写入干扰和 KV 生命周期管理的挑战。HBFlex 通过协调优化 KV 读取、写入和回收来应对这些挑战。

Method: HBFlex 通过协调 KV 放置和注意力访问、聚集增量更新并在计算窗口内安排写回、结合基于生命周期的块打包与延迟回收来减少有效页面迁移。

Result: HBFlex 在不同配置下相对于 FlashAccel 和 H3 的平均吞吐量提高了 1.58 倍和 3.30 倍，展示了更高的 HBF 带宽和更有效的动态 KV 缓存管理带来的益处。

Conclusion: HBFlex 提供了一个全面优化的 HBF 存储系统，解决了全 HBF 运行大型语言模型所面临的主要挑战，通过实验结果证明了其有效性和性能优势。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) require increasing memory capacity to accommodate growing model weights and KV caches. High-Bandwidth Flash (HBF) offers high memory density and aggregate read bandwidth through massive plane-level parallelism, making it an attractive option for LLM serving. However, serving LLMs entirely from HBF introduces three challenges: fine-grained KV reads create placement and access imbalance, incremental writes interfere with foreground reads, and mixed KV lifetimes amplify garbage collection. Hybrid HBM/HBF designs retain HBM to support dynamic KV management, but this allocation reduces the HBF resources available under a fixed packaging budget, limiting aggregate HBF bandwidth.
  We present HBFlex, a full-HBF memory system with coordinated optimizations for KV reads, writes, and reclamation. HBFlex balances KV placement and attention accesses to improve plane utilization. It aggregates incremental updates and schedules writeback within sufficiently long compute windows to reduce write--read interference. It also combines lifetime-guided block packing with deferred reclamation to reduce valid-page migration. We evaluate HBFlex through trace-driven simulation across different configurations. HBFlex achieves average throughput speedups of up to 1.58$\times$ over FlashAccel and 3.30$\times$ over H3, benefiting from higher HBF bandwidth and more efficient management of dynamic KV-cache reads, writes, and erases.

</details>


### [277] [Quantifying the Effect of HCLs on a Fixed-Microarchitecture MXFP4 Accelerator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18792)
*Daniele Passaretti,Sajjad Tamimi,Nicola Dall'Ora*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 该论文使用相同的固定设计（OCP MXFP4块点积）来比较最常用的硬件构造语言（HCL），设计保持不变，验证了这些HCLs在面积和时间性能上可以匹配甚至超过手工编写的RTL代码，差异主要来自后端降低算术的方式和单一宽度选择对DSP推理的影响。因此，选择HCLs主要取决于生态系统适应性和界面需求，而非质量度量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了能够精确地评估不同的硬件构造语言（HCLs）的效果，而非仅仅依赖定性的比较或不同设计的结果质量度量（QoR），作者选择使用同一固定设计进行比较。

Method: 实验选用了OCP MXFP4块点积作为固定设计，并基于Artix-7设备和100 MHz频率进行了相同的流程。每个变种都通过了相同的流水线，评估包括使用SystemVerilog作为基准，Chisel、SpinalHDL、Amaranth、Clash、Bluespec和C++进行HLS实现。

Result: 每种变种都达到了时间要求，并且HCLs的表现达到或超过了手动编写的RTL代码，在面积上具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明，HCLs在关键性能指标上不逊于手动编写的RTL代码，之后的选择将基于生态系统匹配和接口需求，而非结果质量。

Abstract: Hardware Construction Languages (HCLs) aim to improve hardware design productivity while generating register-transfer-level (RTL) circuits without changing the designer's microarchitecture. However, most comparisons between HCLs are either qualitative or evaluate quality of results (QoR) across different designs, making it difficult to separate language effects from design effects. This paper compares the most widely used HCLs using the same fixed design, the OCP MXFP4 block dot product, a quantization primitive at the heart of edge Physical-AI inference, implemented as a single 12-stage, II=1 pipeline. A SystemVerilog baseline is followed by implementations in Chisel, SpinalHDL, Amaranth, Clash, Bluespec, and C++ for high-level synthesis (HLS). Every variant goes through the same flow on the same Artix-7 device set at 100 MhZ, driven by a RISC-V soft core. With the micro-architecture held constant, the comparison is clean: every variant meets timing, and the HCLs match or even undercut hand-written RTL in area. The remaining differences stem not from the algorithm but from how each back end lowers arithmetic, and from a single width choice that silently toggles DSP inference. Unlike HLS, where design decisions are limited to pragmas, the HCLs achieve comparable area and timing. Therefore, the choice comes down to ecosystem fit and interface needs rather than QoR.

</details>


### [278] [Locus: A Framework for Exploring and Optimizing Point Addition Hardware for Zero-Knowledge Proofs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18846)
*Gaurav Kuwar,Alhad Daftardar,Jianqiao Mo,Siddharth Garg,Brandon Reagen*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: Locus框架为优化和探索椭圆曲线点加硬件提供了自动化工具，能够生成ASIC和FPGA实现，并在12nm技术节点上实现了比先前ASIC高达2.71倍的几何平均速度提升和3.11倍的几何平均面积减少，比CPU快34.67倍，并在等效面积下，ZKP加速器的整体证明生成速度提高了3.15倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前硬件实现零知识证明点加操作存在探索空间不足和缺乏系统性指导的问题，Locus旨在解决这一问题，提供了一种自动化的框架来优化和探索点加硬件实现。

Method: Locus框架接受任何受支持方程形式的椭圆曲线参数，自动生成ASIC和FPGA实现。通过Locus进行了一系列探索性的硬件设计研究，涉及超过1000个设计点。

Result: 在12纳米技术节点上，开发的点加硬件设计提供了比之前的ASIC高2.71倍的几何平均速度提升和3.11倍的几何平均面积减少，比CPU快34.67倍，集成到零知识证明加速器中时，等效面积下的整体证明生成速度提高了3.15倍。

Conclusion: Locus框架为椭圆曲线点加的硬件实现提供了有价值的工具，有助于提高零知识证明系统在实际应用中的性能和效率。

Abstract: Zero-Knowledge Proofs (ZKPs) are critical for privacy-preserving and verifiable computation, but their cryptographic primitives impose high computational overheads. One such primitive is point addition (PADD) on elliptic curves. Several prior works have implemented PADDs in hardware, but only for a few specific elliptic curves and design points, leaving a large design space unexplored, and lacking systematic guidance on hardware design trade-offs. To address this gap, we present Locus, a framework dedicated to optimizing and exploring point addition hardware. Given the parameters of any elliptic curve in a supported equation form, Locus automatically generates ASIC and FPGA implementations of PADD, enabling systematic exploration of the PADD design space. Using Locus, we conduct the first comprehensive hardware-focused study of PADD designs, exploring trade-offs over 1,000 design points. On a 12nm technology node, our framework produces PADD designs that yield a $2.71\times$ geomean speedup and $3.11\times$ geomean area reduction compared to prior ASICs, $34.67\times$ geomean speedup over CPU, and $3.15\times$ geomean speedup on end-to-end proof generation when integrated into a prior ZKP accelerator at iso-area. Locus is available at https://github.com/cryptolets/cryptolets/tree/locus.

</details>


### [279] [Rect3D: A Unified Analytical Framework for 3D-IC Rectilinear Floorplanning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18946)
*Shuo Ren,Rongliang Fu,Libo Shen,Zhen Zhuang,Leilei Jin,Chen Wu,Lei He,Bei Yu,Tsung-Yi Ho*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: Rect3D 提出了一种结合概率性die间块分配的统一连续优化模型，解决了3D IC设计优化空间受限的问题。该模型通过图拉普拉斯初始化、可扩展的基于梯度的全局优化过程和3D网格校正方法实现优化，显著减少了线长和运行时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前3D ICs的平面划分方法存在分割阶段与布局阶段的限制，阻碍了设计优化空间的扩展與潜力实现。

Method: Rect3D框架结合了图拉普拉斯初始化、可扩展的基于梯度的全局优化过程和3D网格校正方法，实现了统一的3D优化。

Result: 实验结果表明，与现有的基准方法相比，Rect3D能够降低多达83.6%的线长，并将运行时间降低多达15.98倍。此外，它还在六种附加的分区优先直角方法中始终获得最低的线长。

Conclusion: Rect3D通过对3D空间内的优化，扩大了优化设计空间，提高了3D ICs的性能。

Abstract: 3D-ICs offer significant performance improvements for modern VLSI designs by reducing global interconnect cost. However, conventional 3D floorplanning methods decompose the problem into separate inter-die partitioning and intra-die floorplanning stages, which can restrict the design optimization space and limit the potential gains. Although directly modeling and optimizing in 3D space can mitigate this limitation, the high computational complexity hinders algorithmic efficiency. To address these challenges, we propose \textsc{Rect3D}, an analytical 3D rectilinear floorplanning framework that integrates probabilistic inter-die block assignment into a unified continuous optimization model. The framework combines graph Laplacian initialization for topology-aware seeding, a scalable gradient-based global optimization procedure for joint die assignment and geometric refinement, and a 3D grid-based legalization method for generating connected rectilinear layouts. On GSRC benchmarks, \textsc{Rect3D} reduces wirelength by up to 83.6\% and runtime by up to 15.98$\times$ compared with representative state-of-the-art 3D floorplanning baselines. It also consistently achieves the lowest wirelength among six additional partition-first rectilinear baselines, showing the advantage of preserving die assignment and in-die geometry in a unified 3D optimization flow.

</details>


<div id='cs.PF'></div>

# cs.PF [[Back]](#toc)

### [280] [Optimal Scheduling in Generalized Switch in Heavy Traffic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17735)
*Runhan Xie,Ziv Scully,Rhonda Righter,Izzy Grosof*

Main category: cs.PF

TL;DR: 文章提出了首个确保在广义交换机中高流量下最优平均响应时间的策略，名为Smallest Equalizing Bucket (SEB)策略。SEB策略通过将作业分组到基于持续时间的桶中，并强制实施一种“平衡化”的服务结构，既保持每个桶的平衡，又优先处理最小的作业。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在具有已知作业持续时间的广义交换机中优化平均响应时间的问题尚未解决。因此，作者旨在提出一种策略来解决这个问题。

Method: SEB策略通过将作业分组到基于持续时间的桶中，并强制实施一种“平衡化”的服务结构，既保持每个桶的平衡，又优先处理最小的作业。同时，通过严格的理论证明证明了该策略在高流量下的最优性。

Result: 证明了SEB策略在高流量下的最优性。此外，模拟进一步证实了SEB启发式方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 文章提出了一个有效的广义交换机调度策略，能够优化已知作业持续时间条件下的平均响应时间。

Abstract: The generalized switch is a highly flexible queueing model, covering multiclass, multiserver, and multiresource queueing systems as well as a wide variety of stochastic networks. Although many scheduling policies have been developed for this model, they almost entirely address unknown job duration settings. How to optimally use known job durations in the generalized switch has remained open. Moreover, optimizing mean response time remains open in both settings. We introduce the first policy to guarantee heavy-traffic optimal mean response time in the generalized switch, our Smallest Equalizing Bucket (SEB) policy. The key challenge in designing an optimal scheduling policy is that we must simultaneously prioritize small jobs and also minimize resource waste, all while fitting within the generalized switch's service options. SEB overcomes this challenge by grouping jobs into duration-based buckets and enforcing an "equalizing" service structure that keeps each bucket balanced while still prioritizing the smallest jobs. We prove SEB's heavy-traffic optimality. Simulations further confirm the effectiveness of SEB-inspired heuristics.

</details>
